jf=jg[jm]for jZ=1,4,1 do if jp>#ja then break end jJ[jp]=sF(sq(sk(ja,jp),jf%256))jf,jp=sb(jf/256),jp+1 end end jV=((jV+1))%sP end return si(jJ)end local function jI(jW,jr,jY)return sI(jW,0,jr,jY%sP,sb(jY/sP))end local function jH(jD,jw,ju,jv,Xe)local Xc=jI(Xe,ju,jv)local XS={sq(jw,Xc[1])}for Xs=1,8,1 do XS[Xs+1]=sq(jD[Xs],Xc[Xs+1])end XS[10]=Xc[10]return XS end local function Xj(XX,XA,XG,XL)local XC=jI(XL,XA,XG)return{sq(XX,XC[1]);XC[10]}end local function XB(XR,Xt,Xb,Xk,XF)local Xi=jI(XF,Xb,Xk)return XR[2]==Xi[10]and XR[1]==sq(Xt,Xi[1])end local function XP(XK,Xq,XQ,Xl,Xd)local XO=jI(Xd,XQ,Xl)if XK[10]~=XO[10]or XK[1]~=sq(Xq,XO[1])then return nil end local Xn={}for Xo=1,8,1 do Xn[Xo]=sq(XK[Xo+1],XO[Xo+1])end return Xn end local function XM(Xz)local XT={}for XN=1,#Xz,1 do local Xa=Xz[XN]XT[XN]=sF(Xa%256,sb(Xa/256)%256,sb(Xa/65536)%256,sb(Xa/16777216)%256)end return si(XT)end return{block=sI;crypt=jN;seal=jH,sealAdmission=Xj,admit=XB;resolve=XP;packWords=XM}end))()local XV={1578563075;4195496696;3714656245;1572487600,1587626696;135123839,3267674235;1569106223}local function Xx(Xy,XE,XJ)local Xp=(eS(420102095))local Xg={}local Xm=es[eS(1950188820)][eS(1866817945)]for Xf=1,#Xp,1 do Xg[Xm(Xp,Xf)]=Xf-1 end local XZ=es[eS(1950188820)][eS(390726924)]local AS=0 local As=Ac>1 for Aj=0,4,1 do local AX if Aj<Ac then AX=Xg[Xm(Xy,Xv+Aj)]if not AX then As=false break end else AX=84 end AS=AS*85+AX end if As then local AA=XI(AS/16777216)%256 local AG=XI(AS/65536)%256 local AL=XI(AS/256)%256 local AC=AS%256 if Ac==5 then Xr(Xu,XW(AA,AG,AL,AC))elseif Ac==4 then Xr(Xu,XW(AA,AG,AL))elseif Ac==3 then Xr(Xu,XW(AA,AG))elseif Ac==2 then Xr(Xu,XW(AA))end end Xv=Xv+Ac end return XH(XY(Xu))end local AB={}local Ap=((function(AR,At,Ab,Ak)local AF=Xx(Ab,Ak)local Ai,AP=es[eS(1950188820)][eS(1866817945)],es[eS(1950188820)][eS(2099041446)]local function AK(Aq,AQ)local Al,Ad,AO,An=Ai(Aq,AQ,AQ+3)return((Al+Ad*256)+AO*65536)+An*16777216 end local Ao={}local
